### [1] [The Bureaucracy of Speed: Structural Equivalence Between Memory Consistency Models and Multi-Agent Authorization Revocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09875)
*Vladyslav Parakhin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 针对Agentic执行模式下传统IAM授权失效问题，提出能力相干系统(CCS)，通过状态映射和RCC一致性策略将未授权操作边界从O(v·TTL)降至O(1)，模拟验证RCC在高velocity场景下比TTL租约降低120倍未授权操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IAM的时序假设在Agentic执行模式下失效。60秒撤销窗口在100 ops/tick下允许约6000次未授权API调用，AWS Lambda规模下达60万次。这是相干性问题而非仅延迟问题，现有时序边界策略无法应对。

Method: 定义能力相干系统(CCS)，构建状态映射φ: Σ_MESI → Σ_auth保持转换结构；提出基于释放一致性的RCC策略并证明安全定理D_rcc ≤ n（与agent速度v无关）；在三个业务场景下对四种策略进行tick-based离散事件模拟（每策略10个确定性种子）。

Result: RCC策略在高velocity场景(50 vs 6000次)实现120倍降低，异常触发撤销时达184倍。120次运行零越界，验证了per-capability安全保证。

Conclusion: RCC策略的未授权操作边界与agent速度v解耦，实现O(1)而非O(v·TTL)的缩放特性，为agentic系统的访问控制提供了可证明安全的相干性机制。

Abstract: The temporal assumptions underpinning conventional Identity and Access Management collapse under agentic execution regimes. A sixty-second revocation window permits on the order of $6 \times 10^3$ unauthorized API calls at 100 ops/tick; at AWS Lambda scale, the figure approaches $6 \times 10^5$. This is a coherence problem, not merely a latency problem. We define a Capability Coherence System (CCS) and construct a state-mapping $\varphi : Σ_{\rm MESI} \to Σ_{\rm auth}$ preserving transition structure under bounded-staleness semantics. A safety theorem bounds unauthorized operations for the execution-count Release Consistency-directed Coherence (RCC) strategy at $D_{\rm rcc} \leq n$, independent of agent velocity $v$ -- a qualitative departure from the $O(v \cdot \mathrm{TTL})$ scaling of time-bounded strategies. Tick-based discrete event simulation across three business-contextualised scenarios (four strategies, ten deterministic seeds each) confirms: RCC achieves a $120\times$ reduction versus TTL-based lease in the high-velocity scenario (50 vs. 6,000 unauthorized operations), and $184\times$ under anomaly-triggered revocation. Zero bound violations across all 120 runs confirm the per-capability safety guarantee. Simulation code: https://github.com/hipvlady/prizm

</details>


### [2] [Emotional Modulation in Swarm Decision Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09963)
*David Freire-Obregón*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将描述蜜蜂群体决策的"蜜蜂方程"扩展为基于智能体的模型，将情绪效价(valence)和唤醒度(arousal)作为交互率的调节器，研究情绪感染对共识形成的影响。通过三个场景的模拟，发现情绪调制可以偏置决策结果并改变收敛时间，而非线性放大效应即使在完全对称的情绪条件下也能产生决定性胜利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物和人类群体中的集体决策常通过简单交互规则将微小差异放大为共识。蜜蜂方程虽能描述蜂群巢穴选择决策，但缺乏对情绪维度的建模。该研究旨在将情绪因素（效价和唤醒度）纳入经典蜂群决策模型，以更真实地模拟情绪传染在共识形成中的作用。

Method: 将蜜蜂方程扩展为基于智能体的模型，引入情绪效价（正负）和唤醒度（高低）作为交互率的调制器，实质性地改变招募和交叉抑制参数。智能体显示模拟面部表情，从其效价-唤醒状态映射，实现情绪传染的模拟研究。

Result: 1) 情绪效价和唤醒度的联合作用影响共识结果和速度；2) 唤醒度可在效价匹配时打破平局；3) 存在"雪球效应"——共识在越过中间支持阈值后加速形成。情绪调制可偏置决策结果并改变收敛时间，实质上改变有效招募率和抑制率；内在非线性放大即使在完全对称情绪条件下也能产生决定性胜利。

Conclusion: 研究将经典蜂群决策理论与情感和社会建模相结合，揭示了情绪不对称性和结构临界点如何共同塑造集体结果。提出的框架为研究自然和人工系统中集体选择情绪维度提供了灵活工具。

Abstract: Collective decision-making in biological and human groups often emerges from simple interaction rules that amplify minor differences into consensus. The bee equation, developed initially to describe nest-site selection in honeybee swarms, captures this dynamic through recruitment and inhibition processes. Here, we extend the bee equation into an agent-based model in which emotional valence (positive-negative) and arousal (low-high) act as modulators of interaction rates, effectively altering the recruitment and cross-inhibition parameters. Agents display simulated facial expressions mapped from their valence-arousal states, allowing the study of emotional contagion in consensus formation.
  Three scenarios are explored: (1) the joint effect of valence and arousal on consensus outcomes and speed, (2) the role of arousal in breaking ties when valence is matched, and (3) the "snowball effect" in which consensus accelerates after surpassing intermediate support thresholds. Results show that emotional modulation can bias decision outcomes and alter convergence times by shifting effective recruitment and inhibition rates. At the same time, intrinsic non-linear amplification can produce decisive wins even in fully symmetric emotional conditions.
  These findings link classical swarm decision theory with affective and social modelling, highlighting how both emotional asymmetries and structural tipping points shape collective outcomes. The proposed framework offers a flexible tool for studying the emotional dimensions of collective choice in both natural and artificial systems.

</details>

### [3] [RSH-SpMM: A Row-Structured Hybrid Kernel for Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08734)
*Aiying Li,Jingwei Sun,Han Li,Wence Ji,Guangzhong Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RSH-SpMM是一种针对GPU的稀疏矩阵矩阵乘法(SpMM)框架,通过自适应行分区、RS-Tile表示和局部性感知重排序技术,解决真实世界稀疏矩阵的极度不规则性问题,实现1.27x-6.13x的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏矩阵-矩阵乘法(SpMM)是图分析、科学模拟和稀疏深度学习工作负载中的基础计算,但真实世界稀疏矩阵的极端不规则性导致现有GPU方法无法保持高Tensor Core利用率和稳定吞吐量。

Method: 提出RSH-SpMM框架,包含:1)自适应行分区;2)RS-Tile表示以暴露适合Tensor Core的密集片段;3)针对不规则行的最小开销CUDA执行路径;4)负载均衡混合内核;5)局部性感知重排序以增强结构一致性。

Result: 在多样化稀疏工作负载上的综合评估表明,RSH-SpMM始终优于最先进的SpMM设计,在高度不规则稀疏结构的矩阵上实现了1.27x-6.13x的加速并保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: RSH-SpMM通过行结构混合方法有效解决了GPU上SpMM的不规则稀疏匹配问题,为图分析、科学模拟和稀疏深度学习提供了高效的计算框架。

Abstract: Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication (SpMM) is a fundamental computation in graph analytics, scientific simulation, and sparse deep learning workloads. However, the extreme irregularity of real-world sparse matrices prevents existing GPU-based methods from maintaining high Tensor Core utilization and stable throughput. We present \textbf{RSH-SpMM}, a fine-grained row-structured hybrid SpMM framework designed to better align irregular sparsity with modern GPU execution pipelines. RSH-SpMM introduces adaptive row partitioning and employs the RS-Tile representation to expose Tensor-Core-efficient dense fragments, while processing irregular rows on a minimal-overhead CUDA execution path. It further employs a load-balanced hybrid kernel with locality-aware reordering to enhance structural coherence and sustain high execution efficiency under highly irregular sparsity. Comprehensive evaluations across diverse sparse workloads demonstrate that RSH-SpMM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art SpMM designs, yielding 1.27x - 6.13x acceleration and maintaining robust performance across matrices with highly irregular sparsity structures.

</details>


### [4] [Benchmarking Federated Learning in Edge Computing Environments: A Systematic Review and Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08735)
*Sales Aribe,Gil Nicholas Cagande*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对面向边缘计算的联邦学习技术进行了系统综述和性能评估，从优化策略、通信效率、隐私保护机制和系统架构四个维度对最新方法进行分类，并通过基准数据集对五种领先FL算法进行评估，发现SCAFFOLD准确率最高(0.90)且鲁棒性最好，FedAvg通信和能效最优，同时揭示了数据异构性、能源限制等研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算环境中数据隐私、低延迟和带宽效率的关键需求推动了联邦学习(FL)的发展。

Method: 使用MNIST、CIFAR-10、FEMNIST和Shakespeare等基准数据集，对五种领先的FL算法进行评估，衡量指标包括准确率、收敛时间、通信开销、能源消耗和对非IID数据的鲁棒性。

Result: SCAFFOLD实现最高准确率(0.90)且鲁棒性最强；FedAvg在通信和能效方面表现最佳。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD在准确率(0.90)和鲁棒性方面表现最佳，而FedAvg在通信和能效方面表现最优。研究揭示了数据异构性、能源限制和可重复性等问题，并提出了未来研究议程以促进更健壮和可扩展的边缘智能FL系统。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a transformative approach for distributed machine learning, particularly in edge computing environments where data privacy, low latency, and bandwidth efficiency are critical. This paper presents a systematic review and performance evaluation of FL techniques tailored for edge computing. It categorizes state-of-the-art methods into four dimensions: optimization strategies, communication efficiency, privacy-preserving mechanisms, and system architecture. Using benchmarking datasets such as MNIST, CIFAR-10, FEMNIST, and Shakespeare, it assesses five leading FL algorithms across key performance metrics including accuracy, convergence time, communication overhead, energy consumption, and robustness to non-Independent and Identically Distributed (IID) data. Results indicate that SCAFFOLD achieves the highest accuracy (0.90) and robustness, while Federated Averaging (FedAvg) excels in communication and energy efficiency. Visual insights are provided by a taxonomy diagram, dataset distribution chart, and a performance matrix. Problems including data heterogeneity, energy limitations, and repeatability still exist despite advancements. To enable the creation of more robust and scalable FL systems for edge-based intelligence, this analysis identifies existing gaps and provides an organized research agenda in the future.

</details>


### [5] [Zipage: Maintain High Request Concurrency for LLM Reasoning through Compressed PagedAttention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08743)
*Mengqi Liao,Lu Wang,Chaoyun Zhang,Bo Qiao,Si Qin,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang,Huaiyu Wan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Compressed PagedAttention方法，结合token级KV缓存驱逐与PagedAttention，设计综合调度策略支持前缀缓存和异步压缩，开发了高并发LLM推理引擎Zipage，在数学推理任务上以2.1倍加速达到95%的Full KV性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理成为大型语言模型的主流范式，解码阶段的KV缓存内存瓶颈成为限制高并发服务的关键因素，尽管现有KV缓存驱逐方法解决了内存问题，但大多数方法难以适用于工业级应用。

Method: 提出Compressed PagedAttention方法，将token级KV缓存驱逐与PagedAttention相结合，并设计综合调度策略，支持前缀缓存和异步压缩，基于此开发了高并发LLM推理引擎Zipage。

Result: 在大规模数学推理任务上，Zipage实现了接近Full KV推理引擎95%的性能，同时提供超过2.1倍的加速。

Conclusion: Zipage作为一种高并发LLM推理引擎，通过结合token级KV缓存驱逐与PagedAttention，在大规模数学推理任务上实现了接近Full KV推理引擎95%的性能，同时提供超过2.1倍的加速，证明了压缩分页注意力方法在工业级应用中的可行性。

Abstract: With reasoning becoming the generative paradigm for large language models (LLMs), the memory bottleneck caused by KV cache during the decoding phase has become a critical factor limiting high-concurrency service. Although existing KV cache eviction methods address the memory issue, most of them are impractical for industrial-grade applications. This paper introduces Compressed PagedAttention, a method that combines token-wise KV cache eviction with PagedAttention. We propose a comprehensive scheduling strategy and support prefix caching and asynchronous compression for Compressed PagedAttention. Based on this, we have developed a high-concurrency LLM inference engine, Zipage. On large-scale mathematical reasoning tasks, Zipage achieves around 95\% of the performance of Full KV inference engines while delivering over 2.1$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [6] [Extension of ACETONE C code generator for multi-core architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08744)
*Yanis Aït-Aïssa,Thomas Carle,Sergei Chichin,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出扩展ACETONE框架以生成并行代码，使其支持多核架构，同时形式化定义处理器分配问题并调研现有解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACETONE框架目前仅能生成顺序代码，无法充分利用多核架构的计算能力，且无法满足安全关键系统对性能日益增长的需求。

Method: 1. 形式化定义处理器分配问题；2. 调研现有并行代码生成解决方案；3. 实现调度启发式算法；4. 创建同步机制模板。

Result: 本文为扩展工作奠定理论基础，调研了相关技术方案，确定了实现方向。

Conclusion: 该扩展将使ACETONE支持多核并行代码生成，提升安全关键系统中机器学习应用的执行效率，完整实现包括调度算法和WCET评估将在最终论文中发表。

Abstract: As the industry's interest in machine learning has grown in recent years, some solutions have emerged to safely embed them in safety-critical systems, such as the C code generator ACETONE. However, this framework is limited to generating sequential code, which cannot make most of the multi-core architectures. In this paper, we initiate an extension of ACETONE for the generation of parallel code by formally defining our processor assignment problem and surveying the state of the art on existing solutions. In the final paper, we will introduce the completed extension, including the implementation of the scheduling heuristic, the creation of templates implementing synchronization mechanisms, and an evaluation of the worst-case execution time of the framework's layers.

</details>


### [7] [Serving Compound Inference Systems on Datacenter GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08797)
*Sriram Devata,Rahul Singh,Sarita Adve*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: JigsawServe是一个针对复合推理系统的服务框架，通过自适应选择模型变体和GPU空间分区来联合优化延迟、准确度和GPU资源成本。实验表明其可将最大服务需求提升11.3倍，同时仅消耗43.3%的GPU资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴领域（如XR）的应用构建为复合推理系统，以任务图形式组合多个ML模型。服务这些复合系统面临两个核心问题：1）如何在复合推理系统的不同任务间分配端到端延迟和准确度预算；2）如何为具有不同资源需求的模型有效分配资源。

Method: 提出JigsawServe，首个联合优化延迟、准确度和GPU资源成本的 serving 框架。主要技术包括：1）自适应选择模型变体；2）通过空间分区GPU为复合推理系统的每个任务进行细粒度资源分配。

Result: 分析评估显示：与最接近的先前工作相比，JigsawServe可将最大服务需求（每秒请求数）提升11.3倍。实证评估显示：对于大范围场景，JigsawServe仅消耗43.3%的可用GPU资源即可满足准确度SLO，延迟SLO违规率低于0.6%。每个特性（准确度缩放、GPU空间分区、任务图感知资源预算）都对高效率有重要贡献，牺牲任一特性都会显著降低效率。

Conclusion: JigsawServe证明了联合优化延迟、准确度和资源成本的重要性。GPU空间分区、自适应模型选择和任务图感知资源分配三项技术协同作用，对于实现复合推理系统的高效服务至关重要，缺一不可。

Abstract: Applications in emerging domains such as XR are being built as compound inference systems, where multiple ML models are composed in the form of a task graph to service each request. Serving these compound systems efficiently raises two questions: how to apportion end-to-end latency and accuracy budgets between different tasks in a compound inference system, and how to allocate resources effectively for different models with varying resource requirements. We present JigsawServe, the first serving framework that jointly optimizes for latency, accuracy, and cost in terms of GPU resources by adaptively choosing model variants and performing fine-grained resource allocation by spatially partitioning the GPUs for each task of a compound inference system. Analytical evaluation of a system with a large number of GPUs shows that JigsawServe can increase the maximum serviceable demand (in requests per second) by 11.3x when compared to the closest prior work. Our empirical evaluation shows that for a large range of scenarios, JigsawServe consumes only 43.3% of the available GPU resources while meeting accuracy SLOs with less than 0.6% latency SLO violations. All of the features in JigsawServe contribute to this high efficiency -- sacrificing any one feature of accuracy scaling, GPU spatial partitioning, or task-graph-informed resource budgeting significantly reduces efficiency.

</details>


### [8] [Nezha: A Key-Value Separated Distributed Store with Optimized Raft Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09122)
*Yangyang Wang,Yucong Dong,Ziqian Cheng,Zichen Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nezha是一种分布式存储系统，通过创新性地整合键值分离与Raft共识协议，在保证强一致性的同时实现可扩展的吞吐量。该系统重新设计了操作级持久化策略，并采用分级垃圾回收，显著提升了读写性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前一致分布式键值存储存在关键性能问题：共识协议（如Raft）与底层存储引擎之间的持久化操作存在重叠，导致显著的I/O开销。这限制了系统的吞吐量和性能。

Method: Nezha重新设计操作级持久化策略，并引入分级垃圾回收机制。系统创新性地将键值分离与Raft协议集成，在保留Raft安全属性的前提下，优化数据持久化和读取路径。

Result: 实验结果表明，Nezha在put、get和scan操作上分别实现了460.2%、12.5%和72.6%的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: Nezha通过键值分离与Raft的创新集成，有效解决了共识协议与存储引擎间的I/O重叠问题，在强一致性保证下显著提升了分布式存储系统的综合性能。

Abstract: Distributed key-value stores are widely adopted to support elastic big data applications, leveraging purpose-built consensus algorithms like Raft to ensure data consistency. However, through systematic analysis, we reveal a critical performance issue in such consistent stores, i.e., overlapping persistence operations between consensus protocols and underlying storage engines result in significant I/O overhead. To address this issue, we present Nezha, a prototype distributed storage system that innovatively integrates key-value separation with Raft to provide scalable throughput in a strong consistency guarantee. Nezha redesigns the persistence strategy at the operation level and incorporates leveled garbage collection, significantly improving read and write performance while preserving Raft's safety properties. Experimental results demonstrate that, on average, Nezha achieves throughput improvements of 460.2%, 12.5%, and 72.6% for put, get, and scan operations, respectively.

</details>


### [9] [FedLECC: Cluster- and Loss-Guided Client Selection for Federated Learning under Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08911)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Giovanni Giunta,Mehrdad Hassanzadeh,Aris Anagnostopoulos,Ioannis Chatzigiannakis,Andrea Vitaletti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FedLECC是一种轻量级、集群感知的损失引导客户端选择策略，用于跨设备联邦学习。该方法通过标签分布相似性对客户端进行分组，并优先选择损失较高的集群和客户端，从而选择出少量但信息丰富且多样化的客户端集合。实验表明，在严重标签偏斜条件下，FedLECC可将测试精度提升高达12%，通信轮次减少约22%，通信开销降低高达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨设备联邦学习系统面临严格的通信和参与约束，以及严重的非独立同分布(non-IID)数据问题，这些问题会降低收敛速度和模型质量。由于每轮训练只能选择部分客户端参与，智能客户端选择成为关键的系统挑战。

Method: 提出FedLECC（Federated Learning with Enhanced Cluster Choice）策略，包含三个核心组件：1）基于标签分布相似性对客户端进行聚类；2）优先选择损失较高的集群；3）在集群内优先选择损失较高的客户端。

Result: 在严重标签偏斜条件下，FedLECC相比强基线方法将测试精度提升高达12%，通信轮次减少约22%，通信开销降低高达50%。

Conclusion: 明智的客户端选择可以提高联邦学习工作负载在云边缘系统中的效率和可扩展性，为解决跨设备FL中的非IID数据挑战提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed Artificial Intelligence (AI) across cloud-edge environments by allowing collaborative model training without centralizing data. In cross-device deployments, FL systems face strict communication and participation constraints, as well as strong non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data that degrades convergence and model quality. Since only a subset of devices (a.k.a clients) can participate per training round, intelligent client selection becomes a key systems challenge. This paper proposes FedLECC (Federated Learning with Enhanced Cluster Choice), a lightweight, cluster-aware, and loss-guided client selection strategy for cross-device FL. FedLECC groups clients by label-distribution similarity and prioritizes clusters and clients with higher local loss, enabling the selection of a small yet informative and diverse set of clients. Experimental results under severe label skew show that FedLECC improves test accuracy by up to 12%, while reducing communication rounds by approximately 22% and overall communication overhead by up to 50% compared to strong baselines. These results demonstrate that informed client selection improves the efficiency and scalability of FL workloads in cloud-edge systems.

</details>


### [10] [Accelerating High-Order Finite Element Simulations at Extreme Scale with FP64 Tensor Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09038)
*Jiqun Tu,Ian Karlin,John Camier,Veselin Dobrev,Tzanio Kolev,Stefan Henneking,Omar Ghattas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 首次展示FP64张量核心可直接编程加速大规模有限元科学计算，通过内核融合优化在NVIDIA新型GPU架构上获得显著性能提升和能效增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU加速高阶有限元模拟需要进一步提高效率和计算速度，以满足实际应用和科学洞察所需的高分辨率需求

Method: 将FP64张量核心与内核融合优化相结合，优化MFEM关键内核

Result: 在GH200和GB200上实现最高2倍性能提升和83%能效提升；在Alps系统近10,000 GPU上实现近完美弱扩展效率和90%强扩展效率

Conclusion: FP64 tensor cores可被直接编程用于加速大规模有限元科学计算应用，在GH200和GB200架构上实现高达2倍性能提升和83%能效提升，在Alps系统上近10,000 GPU实现近完美弱扩展效率和90%强扩展效率，直接造福复杂生产代码，包括2025戈登贝尔奖获奖应用。

Abstract: Finite element simulations play a critical role in a wide range of applications, from automotive design to tsunami modeling and computational electromagnetics. Performing these simulations efficiently at the high resolutions needed for practical applications and scientific insights necessitates the use of high-order methods and large-scale supercomputing. While much progress has been made in porting finite element codes to GPU systems in recent years, additional improvements in the efficiency and computational speed of GPU-accelerated high-order finite element simulations are in constant demand. In this paper, we demonstrate that the FP64 tensor cores on NVIDIA GPUs can be used to further accelerate such simulations, achieving significant speedups in key kernels of MFEM, a scalable open-source finite element library widely used in HPC applications. By integrating FP64 tensor cores with kernel fusion optimizations, we were able to achieve up to 2$\times$ performance gains and up to 83% energy efficiency gains on NVIDIA's Grace Hopper GH200 and Grace Blackwell GB200 architectures. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time that FP64 tensor cores have been directly programmed to accelerate large-scale finite element scientific computing applications. We demonstrate the performance of the optimized kernels at exascale by showing near-perfect weak scaling efficiency and 90% strong scaling efficiency across nearly 10,000 GPUs on the Alps system. The new algorithms and MFEM enhancements directly benefit complex production codes, including the 2025 Gordon Bell Prize-winning application for real-time tsunami forecasting.

</details>


### [11] [PIM-SHERPA: Software Method for On-device LLM Inference by Resolving PIM Memory Attribute and Layout Inconsistencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09216)
*Sunjung Lee,Sanghoon Cha,Hyeonsu Kim,Seungwoo Seo,Yuhwan Ro,Sukhan Lee,Byeongho Kim,Yongjun Park,Kyomin Sohn,Seungwon Lee,Jaehoon Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM在移动/边缘设备上部署时PIM系统面临的内存属性和布局不一致问题，提出了PIM-SHERPA软件解决方案，通过DRAM双缓冲和在线权重重排两种方法，在Llama 3.2上实现了约48%的内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在移动和边缘设备上的部署快速增长，但PIM-enabled系统面临两个障碍：1) 内存属性不一致 - prefill阶段希望权重放在可缓存区域以便复用，而decode阶段需要权重在非缓存区域以可靠触发PIM；2) 权重布局不一致 - 主机友好布局与PIM感知布局之间存在差异。

Method: 提出PIM-SHERPA软件方法解决PIM内存属性和布局不一致问题。包含两种方法：1) DRAM双缓冲(DDB) - 在非缓存区域保持单一PIM感知权重，同时将下一层的swizzled权重预取到小型可缓存缓冲区；2) 在线权重重排与swizzled内存复制(OWR) - 在GEMM之前按需执行swizzled内存复制。

Result: 与基线PIM仿真系统相比，PIM-SHERPA在Llama 3.2模型上实现了约47.8-49.7%的内存容量节省，同时保持与理论最大性能相当的性能。

Conclusion: 首次识别了内存属性不一致问题，并为产品级PIM启用系统提出了有效解决方案的软件方法。

Abstract: On-device deployments of large language models (LLMs) are rapidly proliferating across mobile and edge platforms. LLM inference comprises a compute-intensive prefill phase and a memory bandwidth-intensive decode phase, and the decode phase has been widely recognized as well-suited to processing-in-memory (PIM) in both academia and industry. However, practical PIM-enabled systems face two obstacles between these phases, a memory attribute inconsistency in which prefill favors placing weights in a cacheable region for reuse whereas decode requires weights in a non-cacheable region to reliably trigger PIM, and a weight layout inconsistency between host-friendly and PIM-aware layouts. To address these problems, we introduce \textit{PIM-SHERPA}, a software-only method for efficient on-device LLM inference by resolving PIM memory attribute and layout inconsistencies. PIM-SHERPA provides two approaches, DRAM double buffering (DDB), which keeps a single PIM-aware weights in the non-cacheable region while prefetching the swizzled weights of the next layer into small cacheable buffers, and online weight rearrangement with swizzled memory copy (OWR), which performs the on-demand swizzled memory copy immediately before GEMM. Compared to a baseline PIM emulation system, PIM-SHERPA achieves approximately 47.8 - 49.7\% memory capacity savings while maintaining comparable performance to the theoretical maximum on the Llama 3.2 model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to identify the memory attribute inconsistency and propose effective solutions on product-level PIM-enabled systems.

</details>


### [12] [Flash-KMeans: Fast and Memory-Efficient Exact K-Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09229)
*Shuo Yang,Haocheng Xi,Yilong Zhao,Muyang Li,Xiaoze Fan,Jintao Zhang,Han Cai,Yujun Lin,Xiuyu Li,Kurt Keutzer,Song Han,Chenfeng Xu,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将传统离线k-means算法重新定位为在线原语,通过FlashAssign和sort-inverse update两项核心创新解决GPU实现中的IO瓶颈和原子写竞争问题,在H200 GPU上实现最高17.9×端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-means长期以来被视为离线处理原语,作者试图将其重新定位为现代AI系统中的在线原语。现有的GPU实现存在两个关键系统瓶颈:1)赋值阶段因N×K距离矩阵在HBM中的显式物化而导致严重IO瓶颈;2)质心更新阶段因不规则scatter式聚合导致的硬件级原子写竞争而性能受限。

Method: 提出flash-kmeans,包含两个核心内核级创新:1)FlashAssign,将距离计算与在线argmin融合,完全绕过中间内存物化;2)sort-inverse update,通过显式构建逆映射将高竞争原子scatter转换为高带宽的段级局部归约。此外还集成了chunked-stream overlap和cache-aware compile heuristics等算法-系统协同设计。

Result: 在NVIDIA H200 GPU上,flash-kmeans相比最佳基线实现端到端加速最高17.9×,相比工业标准库cuML加速33×,相比FAISS加速超过200×。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将k-means从离线处理原语转变为现代AI系统的在线组件,通过算法-系统协同设计克服了GPU实现中的根本性系统瓶颈,为大规模在线学习场景提供了高效可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: $k$-means has historically been positioned primarily as an offline processing primitive, typically used for dataset organization or embedding preprocessing rather than as a first-class component in online systems. In this work, we revisit this classical algorithm under the lens of modern AI system design and enable $k$-means as an online primitive. We point out that existing GPU implementations of $k$-means remain fundamentally bottlenecked by low-level system constraints rather than theoretical algorithmic complexity. Specifically, the assignment stage suffers from a severe IO bottleneck due to the massive explicit materialization of the $N \times K$ distance matrix in High Bandwidth Memory (HBM). Simultaneously, the centroid update stage is heavily penalized by hardware-level atomic write contention caused by irregular, scatter-style token aggregations. To bridge this performance gap, we propose flash-kmeans, an IO-aware and contention-free $k$-means implementation for modern GPU workloads. Flash-kmeans introduces two core kernel-level innovations: (1) FlashAssign, which fuses distance computation with an online argmin to completely bypass intermediate memory materialization; (2) sort-inverse update, which explicitly constructs an inverse mapping to transform high-contention atomic scatters into high-bandwidth, segment-level localized reductions. Furthermore, we integrate algorithm-system co-designs, including chunked-stream overlap and cache-aware compile heuristics, to ensure practical deployability. Extensive evaluations on NVIDIA H200 GPUs demonstrate that flash-kmeans achieves up to 17.9$\times$ end-to-end speedup over best baselines, while outperforming industry-standard libraries like cuML and FAISS by 33$\times$ and over 200$\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [13] [Case Study: Performance Analysis of a Virtualized XRootD Frontend in Large-Scale WAN Transfers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09568)
*J M da Silva,M A Costa,R L Iope*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文档展示了T2_BR_SPRACE存储前端架构的详细案例研究，该架构由异构XRootD虚拟机集群组成（包含10 Gb/s和40 Gb/s链路），通过pNFS从77 Gb/s dCache后端聚合数据至外部100 Gb/s WAN链路。在峰值生产条件下，系统实现了51.3 Gb/s的聚合吞吐量；向费米实验室（FNAL）的数据传输峰值达41.5 Gb/s，经CERN外部监控工具验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档记录复杂虚拟化架构在真实负载下的性能表现，为高强度数据传输场景提供参考。

Method: 采用异构集群架构（XRootD VMs + dCache后端 + pNFS），配置BBR拥塞控制算法和TCP扩展，在峰值生产条件下进行实际性能测试，并通过CERN外部监控工具验证数据流性能。

Result: 系统 sustained持续聚合吞吐量51.3 Gb/s；向FNAL的数据流峰值达41.5 Gb/s，接近40 Gb/s链路带宽上限。

Conclusion: BBR拥塞控制算法和TCP扩展可使复杂虚拟化存储架构在高强度数据传输中接近物理链路带宽上限，实现高效数据吞吐。

Abstract: This paper presents a detailed case study of the T2_BR_SPRACE storage frontend architecture and its observed performance in high-intensity data transfers. The architecture is composed of a heterogeneous cluster of XRootD [1] Virtual Machines (VMs) with 10 Gb/s and 40 Gb/s links, which aggregate data from a 77 Gb/s dCache [2] backend via pNFS to an external 100 Gb/s WAN link. We describe the system configuration, including the use of the BBR [3] congestion control algorithm and TCP extensions [4]. Under peak production conditions, we observed the system sustaining an aggregate throughput of 51.3 Gb/s. An analysis of a specific data flow to Fermilab (FNAL) showed peaks of 41.5 Gb/s, validated by external monitoring tools (CERN). This study documents the performance of a complex virtualized architecture under real load.

</details>


### [14] [Multi-DNN Inference of Sparse Models on Edge SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09642)
*Jiawei Luo,Di Wu,Simon Dobson,Blesson Varghese*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SparseLoom系统，通过模型拼接(model stitching)技术将稀疏模型的子图重新组合以创建模型变体，无需重新训练即可在异构处理器上部署多DNN推理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘应用需要多DNN推理系统在异构处理器上执行任务，但现有系统仅支持单模型或少量稀疏变体，导致模型与加速器匹配效率低下，产生较高的SLO违规率。

Method: 提出模型拼接技术，通过重新组合稀疏模型的子图来创建模型变体，无需重新训练。实现演示系统SparseLoom，验证该技术可在SoC上部署。

Result: 实验表明SparseLoom相比最先进的多元DNN推理系统：SLO违规率降低最多74%，吞吐量提升最多2.31倍，内存开销平均降低28%。

Conclusion: 模型拼接技术可有效部署于片上系统，能够在多DNN推理场景中显著提升性能并降低资源消耗，为异构边缘计算环境提供高效的模型变体生成方案。

Abstract: Modern edge applications increasingly require multi-DNN inference systems to execute tasks on heterogeneous processors, gaining performance from both concurrent execution and from matching each model to the most suited accelerator. However, existing systems support only a single model (or a few sparse variants) per task, which impedes the efficiency of this matching and results in high Service Level Objective violation rates. We introduce model stitching for multi-DNN inference systems, which creates model variants by recombining subgraphs from sparse models without re-training. We present a demonstrator system, SparseLoom, that shows model stitching can be deployed to SoCs. We show experimentally that SparseLoom reduces SLO violation rates by up to 74%, improves throughput by up to 2.31x, and lowers memory overhead by an average of 28% compared to state-of-the-art multi-DNN inference systems.

</details>


### [15] [Configurable Runtime Orchestration for Dynamic Data Retrieval in Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06980)
*Abhiram Kandiraju*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种配置驱动的运行时编排框架，用于分布式系统中的动态数据检索。该框架在请求时根据配置动态生成执行图，实现低延迟编排，支持集成变化时无需重新部署工作流代码即可适应。通过依赖感知调度和并行执行实现高效聚合，并通过客户360检索案例验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业平台依赖分布式微服务、分析数据平台和外部API构建复合响应。传统工作流平台（如Apache Airflow、AWS Step Functions、Temporal）需要在执行前定义工作流，难以适应集成快速演化的需求。编排跨异构系统的数据检索具有挑战性。

Method: 提出一个配置驱动的运行时编排框架，在请求时根据配置动态生成执行图。执行计划器执行依赖感知的调度和独立任务的并行执行，实现跨分布式服务的高效聚合。

Result: 框架实现了低延迟编排，支持动态配置更新无需重新部署工作流代码，通过依赖感知调度和并行执行提高效率。客户360检索案例展示了该方法在企业级场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过运行时配置实现了灵活、可扩展的数据编排，能够适应快速变化的集成环境，特别适用于需要频繁更新集成点的企业场景。无需重新部署工作流代码即可适应集成变化，降低了运维成本并提高了响应速度。

Abstract: Modern enterprise platforms increasingly depend on distributed microservices, analytical data platforms, and external APIs to construct composite responses for applications. Orchestrating data retrieval across these heterogeneous systems is challenging because many workflow platforms rely on predefined workflows or state-machine definitions. Systems such as Apache Airflow, AWS Step Functions, and Temporal provide powerful orchestration capabilities but typically assume workflows are defined prior to execution. This paper presents a configuration-driven runtime orchestration framework for dynamic data retrieval in distributed systems. The framework generates execution graphs dynamically from configuration at request time, enabling low-latency orchestration without redeploying workflow code when integrations evolve. The execution planner performs dependency-aware scheduling and parallel execution of independent tasks, allowing efficient aggregation across distributed services. The paper describes the architecture, execution model, and operational tradeoffs of this framework, and presents a representative enterprise case study for Customer 360 retrieval. The approach demonstrates how runtime configuration can enable flexible and scalable orchestration in rapidly evolving integration environments.

</details>

### [16] [GenAI Is No Silver Bullet for Qualitative Research in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08951)
*Neil A. Ernst,Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于软件工程领域中定性研究的前沿论文，探讨了大型语言模型和生成式AI在定性分析中的应用。作者批判了AI可以自动化定性分析的说法过于笼统，阐述了GenAI支持需要根据具体研究策略和数据特征进行适配。文章从定性工作的维度、GenAI辅助的实证证据、GenAI介导的定性研究实践的优缺点，以及GenAI背景下定性研究的质量因素等方面进行了综述，旨在帮助研究人员了解GenAI辅助定性研究的承诺与陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，大型语言模型和生成式AI（GenAI）的发展引发了一种说法，认为人工智能可以自动化定性分析。然而，这种说法是基于狭窄成功案例的过度概括。作者认为GenAI支持必须根据具体的研究策略和数据特征进行适当调整，因此在软件工程定性研究的前沿领域，需要重新审视GenAI的应用。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，从以下维度展开讨论：1）概述软件工程中定性工作的维度；2）回顾GenAI辅助的现有实证证据；3）审视GenAI介导的定性研究实践的优缺点；4）在GenAI背景下重新探讨定性研究的质量因素。

Result: 本文为研究人员提供了关于GenAI辅助定性研究的承诺与陷阱的系统性认知，涵盖软件工程定性研究的广泛频谱，包括从解释主义的实地观察研究到挖掘研究的演绎编码等不同策略。

Conclusion: 作者总结了GenAI在软件工程定性研究中的应用前景与挑战，并提出了推进理解GenAI在该领域使用的未来计划。

Abstract: Qualitative research gives rich insights into the quintessentially human aspects of software engineering as a socio-technical system. Qualitative research spans diverse strategies and methods, from interpretivist, in situ observational field studies, to deductive coding of data from mining studies. Advances in large language models and generative AI (GenAI) have prompted claims that artificial intelligence could automate qualitative analysis. Such claims are overgeneralizing from narrow successes. GenAI support must be carefully adapted to the data of interest, but also to the characteristics of a particular research strategy. In this Frontiers of SE paper, we discuss the emerging use of GenAI in relation to the broad spectrum of qualitative research in software engineering. We outline the dimensions of qualitative work in software engineering, review emerging empirical evidence for GenAI assistance, examine the pros and cons of GenAI-mediated qualitative research practices, and revisit qualitative research quality factors, in light of GenAI. Our goal is to inform researchers about the promises and pitfalls of GenAI-assisted qualitative research. We conclude with future plans to advance understanding of its use in software engineering.

</details>

*Tony Mason*

Main category: cs.SE

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: System prompts for LLM-based coding agents are software artifacts that govern agent behavior, yet lack the testing infrastructure applied to conventional software. We present Arbiter, a framework combining formal evaluation rules with multi-model LLM scouring to detect interference patterns in system prompts. Applied to three major coding agent system prompts: Claude Code (Anthropic), Codex CLI (OpenAI), and Gemini CLI (Google), we identify 152 findings across the undirected scouring phase and 21 hand-labeled interference patterns in directed analysis of one vendor. We show that prompt architecture (monolithic, flat, modular) strongly correlates with observed failure class but not with severity, and that multi-model evaluation discovers categorically different vulnerability classes than single-model analysis. One scourer finding was structural data loss in Gemini CLI's memory system was consistent with an issue filed and patched by Google, which addressed the symptom without addressing the schema-level root cause identified by the scourer. Total cost of cross-vendor analysis: \$0.27 USD.

### [18] [Automating Detection and Root-Cause Analysis of Flaky Tests in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09029)
*Janakan Sivaloganathan,Ainaz Jamshidi,Andriy Miranskyy,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种自动化流水线，使用LLM检测量子软件仓库中的flaky测试并识别其根因，成功扩展数据集54%，Gemini模型在分类任务中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件 inherently具有概率性输出，容易产生flaky tests（间歇性通过/失败的测试），这会掩盖真实缺陷并降低开发者生产力，但目前缺乏系统的检测和诊断工具。

Method: 使用LLM和余弦相似度自动发现flaky测试用例；评估了OpenAI GPT、Meta LLaMA、Google Gemini和Anthropic Claude等多种LLM进行flakiness分类和根因识别；使用F1-score等标准指标评估分类性能。

Result: 识别出25个先前未知的flaky测试，使原始数据集增加54%。最佳模型Google Gemini在flakiness检测上达到F1-score 0.9420，在根因识别上达到0.9643。

Conclusion: LLMs能够有效支持量子软件中flaky测试的分类和根因识别，Gemini模型表现最佳。扩展的数据集和自动化流水线为量子软件工程社区提供了可复用的资源。

Abstract: Like classical software, quantum software systems rely on automated testing. However, their inherently probabilistic outputs make them susceptible to quantum flakiness -- tests that pass or fail inconsistently without code changes. Such quantum flaky tests can mask real defects and reduce developer productivity, yet systematic tooling for their detection and diagnosis remains limited.
  This paper presents an automated pipeline to detect flaky-test-related issues and pull requests in quantum software repositories and to support the identification of their root causes. We aim to expand an existing quantum flaky test dataset and evaluate the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) for flakiness classification and root-cause identification.
  Building on a prior manual analysis of 14 quantum software repositories, we automate the discovery of additional flaky test cases using LLMs and cosine similarity. We further evaluate a variety of LLMs from OpenAI GPT, Meta LLaMA, Google Gemini, and Anthropic Claude suites for classifying flakiness and identifying root causes from issue descriptions and code context. Classification performance is assessed using standard performance metrics, including F1-score.
  Using our pipeline, we identify 25 previously unknown flaky tests, increasing the original dataset size by 54%. The best-performing model, Google Gemini, achieves an F1-score of 0.9420 for flakiness detection and 0.9643 for root-cause identification, demonstrating that LLMs can provide practical support for triaging flaky reports and understanding their underlying causes in quantum software.
  The expanded dataset and automated pipeline provide reusable artifacts for the quantum software engineering community. Future work will focus on improving detection robustness and exploring automated repair of quantum flaky tests.

</details>


### [19] [The Future of Software Engineering Conferences: A New Zealand Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09035)
*Kelly Blincoe,Sherlock A. Licorish,Judith Fuchs,Amjed Tahir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究软件工程会议对新西兰等地理遥远地区研究人员的参与障碍，提出混合参与、成本节约场馆和治理改革等策略，以促进全球公平参与

Method: 识别并分析地理遥远地区研究人员参与SE会议的具体障碍，提出包括混合参与模式、成本意识场馆选择以及治理改革等策略

Result: 提出促进公平全球参与的建议，以加强SE研究社区的包容性和协作

Conclusion: 通过混合参与、成本控制措施和治理改革，可以有效降低地理障碍，提升全球SE研究社区的公平性和参与度

Abstract: Software engineering (SE) conferences are vital for knowledge exchange and collaboration, yet can also involve significant barriers for researchers in geographically distant regions such as New Zealand. We identify barriers such as high travel costs, misaligned academic calendars, and limited representation, and propose strategies including hybrid participation, cost-conscious venues, and governance reforms. We make recommendations to promote equitable global participation and strengthen the SE research community.

</details>


### [20] [Class Model Generation from Requirements using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09100)
*Jackson Nguyen,Rui En Koe,Fanyu Wang,Chetan Arora,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型(LLM)从自然语言需求自动生成UML类图的能力，评估了GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 4.0、Gemini 2.5 Flash Thinking和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct四种模型，提出结合LLM-as-Judge与人工评估的双重验证框架，在8个数据集上从五个维度进行评估，结果显示LLM可生成结构一致、语义准确的UML图，且LLM评估与人工评估高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类图生成是软件设计中的关键但资源密集型任务，传统方法需要大量人工 effort。LLM的出现为自动化软件工程活动提供了新机会，但LLM在UML类图自动生成方面的有效性和可靠性尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 1) 提出了双重验证框架：LLM-as-Judge + human-in-the-loop；2) 使用8个异构数据集；3) 采用chain-of-thought prompting提取领域实体、属性和关联，生成PlantUML表示；4) 从五个维度评估：完整性、正确性、标准遵循性、可理解性、术语一致性；5) 使用Grok和Mistral作为LLM法官进行结构化成对比较，并与专家评估对比验证。

Result: LLM能够生成结构一致、语义有意义的UML类图；LLM评估与人工评估达到高度一致，表明LLM不仅可作为建模助手，还可作为自动化需求工程工作流中的可靠评估器。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的UML类图自动化具有实践可行性，LLM在建模和评估任务中均表现出潜力，为自动化需求工程提供了有价值的见解，同时揭示了当前方法的能力与局限性。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has opened new opportunities to automate software engineering activities that traditionally require substantial manual effort. Among these, class diagram generation represents a critical yet resource-intensive phase in software design. This paper investigates the capabilities of state-of-the-art LLMs, including GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 4.0, Gemini 2.5 Flash Thinking, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, to generate UML class diagrams from natural language requirements automatically. To evaluate the effectiveness and reliability of LLM-based model generation, we propose a comprehensive dual-validation framework that integrates an LLM-as-a-Judge methodology with human-in-the-loop assessment. Using eight heterogeneous datasets, we apply chain-of-thought prompting to extract domain entities, attributes, and associations, generating corresponding PlantUML representations. The resulting models are evaluated across five quality dimensions: completeness, correctness, conformance to standards, comprehensibility, and terminological alignment. Two independent LLM judges (Grok and Mistral) perform structured pairwise comparisons, and their judgments are further validated against expert evaluations. Our results demonstrate that LLMs can generate structurally coherent and semantically meaningful UML diagrams, achieving substantial alignment with human evaluators. The consistency observed between LLM-based and human-based assessments highlights the potential of LLMs not only as modeling assistants but also as reliable evaluators in automated requirements engineering workflows, offering practical insights into the capabilities and limitations of LLM-driven UML class diagram automation.

</details>


### [21] [Can ChatGPT Generate Realistic Synthetic System Requirement Specifications? Results of a Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09335)
*Alex R. Mattukat,Florian M. Braun,Horst Lichter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究使用ChatGPT生成合成系统需求规格说明（SSyRS），通过提示模式和迭代优化生成300个样本。62%专家认為逼真，但存在矛盾和缺陷，证明LLM质量评估无法完全替代专家评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统需求规格说明（SyRS）是关键的自然语言工件，但获取真实SyRS受限于专有限制或保密问题。生成合成SyRS（SSyRS）可解决这一稀缺问题。

Method: 使用提示模式、基于LLM的质量评估和迭代提示优化，系统性生成300个跨10个行业的SSyRS。通过跨模型检查和专家研究（n=87份问卷）评估结果。

Result: 62%的专家认为SSyRS是逼真的，但深入检查发现存在矛盾陈述和缺陷。LLM质量评估无法完全替代专家评估。

Conclusion: 可以一定程度生成逼真的SSyRS，但LLM质量评估无法完全替代专家评估。研究揭示了LLM生成内容在真实性和一致性方面的问题。

Abstract: System requirement specifications (SyRSs) are central, natural-language (NL) artifacts. Access to real SyRS for research purposes is highly valuable but limited by proprietary restrictions or confidentiality concerns. Generating synthetic SyRSs (SSyRSs) can address this scarcity. Black-box large language models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT offer compelling generation capabilities by providing easy access to NL generation functions without requiring access to real data. However, LLMs suffer from hallucinations and overconfidence, which pose major challenges in their use. We designed an exploratory study to investigate whether, despite these challenges, we can generate realistic SSyRSs with ChatGPT without having access to real SyRSs. Using a systematic approach that leverages prompt patterns, LLM-based quality assessments, and iterative prompt refinements, we generated 300 SSyRSs across 10 industries with ChatGPT. The results were evaluated using cross-model checks and an expert study, with n=87 submitted surveys. 62\% of experts considered the SSyRSs to be realistic. However, in-depth examination revealed contradictory statements and deficiencies. Overall, we were able to generate realistic SSyRSs to a certain extent with ChatGPT, but LLM-based quality assessments cannot fully replace thorough expert evaluations. This paper presents the methodology and results of our study and discusses the key insights we obtained.

</details>


### [22] [Declarative Scenario-based Testing with RoadLogic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09455)
*Ezio Bartocci,Alessio Gambi,Felix Gigler,Cristinel Mateis,Dejan Ničković*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RoadLogic，一个连接声明式OpenSCENARIO规范与可执行仿真的开源工具。它使用Answer Set Programming生成满足场景约束的抽象计划，通过运动规划将计划精化为可行轨迹，并使用基于规范的监控验证正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于场景的测试是自动驾驶汽车验证的关键方法。现有方法依赖命令式场景定义，需要开发者手动列举大量变体以实现覆盖。声明式语言如OS2提高了抽象层次，但缺乏将规范实例化为符合规范的仿真场景的系统性方法，且目前没有开源解决方案。

Method: RoadLogic系统包含三个核心组件：1) 使用Answer Set Programming生成满足场景约束的抽象计划；2) 使用运动规划将抽象计划精化为可行轨迹；3) 使用基于规范的监控验证正确性。

Result: 在CommonRoad框架上实例化代表性OS2场景进行评估。结果表明RoadLogic能在数分钟内持续生成符合规范的现实仿真，并通过参数采样捕获多样行为变体。

Conclusion: RoadLogic填补了声明式场景规范与可执行仿真之间的空白，为自动驾驶系统的系统性基于场景测试开辟了道路。

Abstract: Scenario-based testing is a key method for cost-effective and safe validation of autonomous vehicles (AVs). Existing approaches rely on imperative scenario definitions, requiring developers to manually enumerate numerous variants to achieve coverage. Declarative languages, such as OpenSCENARIO DSL (OS2), raise the abstraction level but lack systematic methods for instantiating concrete, specification-compliant scenarios as simulations. To our knowledge, currently, no open-source solution provides this capability.
  We present RoadLogic that bridges declarative OS2 specifications and executable simulations. It uses Answer Set Programming to generate abstract plans satisfying scenario constraints, motion planning to refine the plans into feasible trajectories, and specification-based monitoring to verify correctness.
  We evaluate RoadLogic on instantiating representative OS2 scenarios as simulations in the CommonRoad framework. Results show that RoadLogic consistently produces realistic, specification-satisfying simulations within minutes and captures diverse behavioral variants through parameter sampling, thus opening the door to systematic scenario-based testing for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [23] [Experience Report on the Adaptable Integration of Requirements Engineering Courses into Curricula for Professionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09467)
*Oleksandr Kosenkov,Konstantin Blaschke,Tony Gorschek,Michael Unterkalmsteiner,Oleksandr Adamov,Davide Fucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了为专业人士开发软件工程教育(SEE)课程并将需求工程(RE)课程整合到其中的实践经验，提出了整合的基本原则和基于课程内容映射的系统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于技术领域不断发展和实践所需技能的变化，对专业人士的软件工程教育需求日益增长。将需求工程课程系统有效地整合到针对专业人士的SEE课程中面临挑战，因为这类课程需求不同、更加动态化和模块化。

Method: 研究报告了三个针对专业人士的SEE课程开发经验，以及将RE课程整合到这些课程中的实践。作者提出了整合的基本原则，并描述了基于课程内容映射的系统方法。

Result: 文章给出了整合RE课程到SEE专业课程的基本原则和系统方法，为其他教育机构提供了可借鉴的经验。

Conclusion: 为专业人士的SEE课程中整合RE课程提供了一套系统性的方法和建议，有助于满足行业对具备需求工程能力的专业人才的需求。

Abstract: There is a growing demand for software engineering education (SEE) for professionals because of the increasing demand, active evolution of the technological landscape, and changes in the skills required by the practice. Integrating requirements engineering (RE) courses into SEE curricula for professionals systematically and effectively is challenging. In particular, curricula for professionals have different demands, are more dynamic, and modular in nature. In this study, we report on our experience in the development of three SEE curricula for professionals and the integration of RE courses into such curricula. We suggest basic principles for such integration and describe the systematic approach focused on course content mapping that we have developed.

</details>


### [24] [Towards Viewpoint-centric Artifact-based Regulatory Requirements Engineering for Compliance by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09492)
*Oleksandr Kosenkov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一篇博士研究中期论文，探讨软件工程中的监管需求工程(RE)问题。研究指出监管RE具有特殊性，组织通常建立独立的监管RE流程，但软件团队以临时方式处理合规。文章提出AM4RRE模型以实现集成合规设计，并寻求反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着法规数量、复杂性和范围的持续增长，软件工程中的监管合规需求处理已成为一项日益严峻的挑战。尽管研究社区提出了许多监管需求工程(RE)方法，但行业仍面临将这些方法整合到RE和整体软件开发生命周期(SDLC)实践中的压力，以实现无缝的合法合规设计。目前关于如何实现这种整合的实证理解仍然有限。

Method: 基于前期研究成果进行综合分析，提出监管需求工程工件模型(AM4RRE)。研究方法包括对监管RE特殊性的分析、组织级监管RE流程的考察，以及软件团队合规设计实践的调研。

Result: 中期研究结果表明，监管需求工程具有不同于其他需求工程的特殊性。组织通常在组织层面建立独立的监管RE流程，但软件开发团队通常以临时方式而非系统方式实现合规设计，部分原因是涉及观点之间协调的复杂性。研究者提出了AM4RRE模型用于集成合规设计。

Conclusion: 该论文处于博士研究的中期阶段，旨在报告AM4RRE模型的合成并寻求反馈，以完善最终研究计划。研究者希望通过本文引发关于监管需求工程的讨论，并帮助完善博士研究的最终阶段计划。

Abstract: Processing regulations and resulting requirements to achieve regulatory compliance in software engineering (SE) is a developing challenge due to the continuously growing amount, complexity, and expanding scope of regulations. Despite the growing amount of newly suggested regulatory requirements engineering (RE) approaches by the research community, industry remains under pressure to assure their integration into their RE and overall software development life cycle (SDLC) practices to facilitate a seamless and legally valid compliance by design. As of today, we still have limited empirical understanding of how this can be achieved. Such integration should avoid additional burdens and address the demands of legal knowledge intensity, cross-functional communication and consistency between different involved viewpoints. Intermediary results of this doctoral study showed that regulatory RE has peculiarities distinguishing it from the engineering of other requirements. Oftentimes, organizations establish standalone regulatory RE processes on the organizational level. However, software development teams usually approach compliance by design in an ad-hoc manner, rather than in a systematic way. Among other, because of the complexity of the coordination between the involved viewpoints. The goal of this paper is to report and get feedback about the synthesis and future evaluation of our Artefact Model for Regulatory Requirements Engineering (AM4RRE) for a integrated compliance by design. We hope this paper will spark discussions about regulatory RE and help us refine plans for the final stage of the doctoral study.

</details>


### [25] [EmbC-Test: How to Speed Up Embedded Software Testing Using LLMs and RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09497)
*Maximilian Harnot,Sebastian Komarnicki,Michal Polok,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强生成(RAG)的管道，用于部分自动化嵌入式C软件的验证过程，通过将大型语言模型扎根于项目特定工件来减少幻觉并提高项目对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式C软件的自动测试手动开发是一项艰巨且耗时的任务，扩展性差。随着软件发布周期加速，验证越来越成为嵌入式开发工作流程中的瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种基于检索增强生成(RAG)的管道，通过将大型语言模型扎根于项目特定工件来减少幻觉并提高项目对齐度。工业评估表明生成的测试100%语法正确，85%成功通过运行时验证。

Result: 生成的测试100%语法正确，85%成功通过运行时验证；潜在可节省高达66%的测试时间；每小时可生成270个测试。

Conclusion: 所提出的RAG管道有潜力将测试时间减少高达66%，同时每小时生成270个测试，证明了在嵌入式C软件验证中结合检索增强生成的有效性。

Abstract: Manual development of automatic tests for embedded C software is a strenuous and time-consuming task that does not scale well. With the accelerating pace of software release cycles, verification increasingly becomes the bottleneck in the embedded development workflow. This paper presents a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline as a solution for partial automation of the verification process. By grounding a large language model in project-specific artifacts, the approach reduces hallucinations and improves project alignment. An industrial evaluation showed that the generated tests are 100 % syntactically correct, with 85 % successfully passing runtime validation. The proposed solution has the potential to save up to 66 % of the testing time compared to manual test writing while generating 270 tests per hour.

</details>


### [26] [An Empirical Study of Interaction Smells in Multi-Turn Human-LLM Collaborative Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09701)
*Binquan Zhang,Li Zhang,Lin Shi,Song Wang,Yuwei Qian,Linhui Zhao,Fang Liu,An Fu,Yida Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对人类-LLM代码生成任务中的交互质量问题进行研究，建立了首个交互异味分类法，涵盖用户意图质量、历史指令遵守和历史响应违规三个类别共9种子类型，并提出InCE多智能体框架通过全局不变式提取和预生成质量审计来改善多轮交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注最终输出的功能正确性，忽视了交互过程中潜在的质量问题（称为"交互异味"）。LLM在生成长代码片段方面表现出色，但在长时间交互中难以保持上下文一致性。

Method: 1）从WildChat和LMSYS-Chat-1M数据集中抽样真实用户-LLM交互进行实证研究；2）通过开放卡片排序建立交互异味分类法；3）对6个主流LLM进行定量评估分析交互异味分布；4）提出InCE多智能体框架进行缓解

Result: 1）建立了首个交互异味分类法，分为三个主要类别（用户意图质量、历史指令遵守、历史响应违规）共9个子类别；2）发现不同模型间交互异味的分布差异；3）InCE框架显著提高任务成功率并有效抑制交互异味

Conclusion: 提出Invariant-aware Constraint Evolution (InCE)多智能体框架，通过显式提取全局不变式和预生成质量审计来改善多轮交互质量。在扩展的WildBench基准测试中，该轻量级缓解方法显著提高了任务成功率并有效抑制了交互异味的产生。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized code generation, evolving from static tools into dynamic conversational interfaces that facilitate complex, multi-turn collaborative programming. While LLMs exhibit remarkable proficiency in generating standalone code snippets, they often struggle to maintain contextual consistency during extended interactions, creating significant obstacles in the collaboration process. Existing benchmarks primarily emphasize the functional correctness of the final output, overlooking latent quality issues within the interaction process itself, which we term Interaction Smells. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study on sampled real-word user-LLM interactions from WildChat and LMSYS-Chat-1M datasets to systematically investigate Interaction Smells in human-LLM code generation tasks from the perspectives of phenomena, distribution, and mitigation. First, we establish the first taxonomy of Interaction Smells by manually performing open card sorting on real-world interaction logs. This taxonomy categorizes Interaction Smells into three primary categories, i.e., User Intent Quality, Historical Instruction Compliance, and Historical Response Violation, comprising nine specific subcategories. Next, we quantitatively evaluate six mainstream LLMs (i.e., GPT-4o, DeepSeek-Chat, Gemini 2.5, Qwen2.5-32B, Qwen2.5-72B, and Qwen3-235B-a22b) to analyze the distribution of Interaction Smells across different models. Finally, we propose Invariant-aware Constraint Evolution (InCE), a multi-agent framework designed to improve multi-turn interaction quality through explicit extraction of global invariants and pre-generation quality audits. Experimental results on the extended WildBench benchmark demonstrate that this lightweight mitigation approach significantly improves the Task Success Rate and effectively suppresses the occurrence of Interaction Smells.

</details>

### [27] [OptBench: An Interactive Workbench for AI/ML-SQL Co-Optimization[Extended Demonstration Proposal]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08880)
*Jaykumar Tandel,Douglas Oscarson,Jia Zou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OptBench是一个交互式工作台,用于构建和基准测试混合SQL+AI/ML查询的查询优化器,在统一的后端(DuckDB)上运行所有优化器,提供交互式Web界面用于构建优化器、基准测试比较和可视化逻辑计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合SQL+ML工作负载难以优化,因为ML运算符像黑盒、数据依赖效应占主导地位、依赖启发式方法、引入大量协同优化机会导致搜索空间显著扩大。同时,SQL+ML优化的研究原型难以公平评估,因为它们通常在不同平台上开发并使用不同查询进行评估。

Method: 提出OptBench系统:(1)使用统一后端DuckDB运行所有优化器实现公平比较;(2)提供交互式Web界面,允许用户利用和扩展抽象的逻辑计划重写操作来构建优化器;(3)支持对多样化查询套件进行基准测试和比较,记录决策追踪和延迟;(4)并排可视化不同优化器产生的逻辑计划。

Result: 系统使研究人员和实践者能够在单一工作台内原型化优化器想法、检查计划转换,并在多模态推理查询上定量比较优化器设计。

Conclusion: OptBench为混合SQL+AI/ML查询优化器的开发、评估和比较提供了一个透明、"苹果对苹果"的基准测试平台,解决了该领域研究中原型评估困难的问题。

Abstract: Database workloads are increasingly nesting artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) pipelines and AI/ML model inferences with data processing, yielding hybrid SQL+AI/ML queries that mix relational operators with expensive, opaque AI/ML operators, often expressed as UDFs. These workloads are challenging to optimize because ML operators behave like black boxes, data-dependent effects such as sparsity, selectivity, and cardinalities can dominate runtime, domain experts often rely on practical heuristics that are difficult to develop with monolithic optimizers, and AI/ML operators introduce numerous co-optimization opportunities such as factorization, pushdown, ML-to-SQL conversion, and linear-algebra-to-relational-algebra rewrites, significantly enlarging the search space of equivalent execution plans. At the same time, research prototypes for SQL+ML optimization are difficult to evaluate fairly because they are typically developed on different platforms and evaluated using different queries.
  We present OptBench, an interactive workbench for building and benchmarking query optimizers for hybrid SQL+AI/ML queries in a transparent, apples-to-apples manner. OptBench runs all optimizers on a unified backend using DuckDB and exposes an interactive web interface that allows users to (i) construct query optimizers by leveraging and extending abstracted logical plan rewrite actions, (ii) benchmark and compare different optimizer implementations over a suite of diverse queries while recording decision traces and latency, and (iii) visualize logical plans produced by different optimizers side-by-side. The system enables practitioners and researchers to prototype optimizer ideas, inspect plan transformations, and quantitatively compare optimizer designs on multimodal inference queries within a single workbench.

</details>


### [28] [The Virtuous Cycle: AI-Powered Vector Search and Vector Search-Augmented AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09347)
*Jiuqi Wei,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本教程论文探讨AI与向量搜索的相互促进作用，建立"良性循环"框架：AI通过学习索引、自适应剪枝和自动化调优提升向量搜索；向量搜索则通过RAG等范式扩展AI能力，缓解LLM知识时效性和幻觉问题。论文发表于ICDE 2026。]


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与向量搜索正在快速融合，形成智能信息系统的前沿研究领域。一方面，AI进展显著提升了向量搜索的语义准确性和效率；另一方面，强大的向量搜索技术支撑了新型AI范式，尤其是检索增强生成(RAG)，有效缓解了大语言模型的知识时效性和幻觉问题。这种相互强化形成了"良性循环"。

Method: 教程采用系统性方法，从三个维度分析AI与向量搜索的融合：(1) AI4VS：探讨AI如何赋能向量搜索管道各环节，包括学习索引结构、自适应剪枝策略、自动化参数调优；(2) VS4AI：研究向量搜索如何支持新型AI范式，重点关注RAG框架的动态外部知识整合；(3) 端到端协同优化策略，释放两者结合的潜力。

Result: 本教程提供了该交叉领域的全面概述，涵盖：(1) AI赋能向量搜索的各个方面；(2) 向量搜索支持的RAG框架及动态知识整合机制；(3) 端到端协同优化策略；(4) 关键挑战和未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 本教程全面概述了AI与向量搜索交叉领域的最新研究进展，建立了"良性循环"框架：AI为向量搜索注入智能和自适应优化，而向量搜索则扩展了AI在知识集成和上下文感知生成方面的能力。为未来的研究指明了方向，包括端到端协同优化、动态知识整合等挑战与机遇。

Abstract: Modern AI and vector search are rapidly converging, forming a promising research frontier in intelligent information systems. On one hand, advances in AI have substantially improved the semantic accuracy and efficiency of vector search, including learned indexing structures, adaptive pruning strategies, and automated parameter tuning. On the other hand, powerful vector search techniques have enabled new AI paradigms, notably Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), which effectively mitigates challenges in Large Language Models (LLMs) like knowledge staleness and hallucinations. This mutual reinforcement establishes a virtuous cycle where AI injects intelligence and adaptive optimization into vector search, while vector search, in turn, expands AI's capabilities in knowledge integration and context-aware generation. This tutorial provides a comprehensive overview of recent research and advancements at this intersection. We begin by discussing the foundational background and motivations for integrating vector search and AI. Subsequently, we explore how AI empowers vector search (AI4VS) across each step of the vector search pipeline. We then investigate how vector search empowers AI (VS4AI), with a particular focus on RAG frameworks that integrate dynamic, external knowledge sources into the generative process of LLMs. Furthermore, we analyze end-to-end co-optimization strategies that fully unlock the potential of the ``virtuous cycle" between vector search and AI. Finally, we highlight key challenges and future research opportunities in this emerging area. This paper was published in ICDE 2026.

</details>


### [29] [GeoBenchr: An Application-Centric Benchmarking Suite for Spatiotemporal Database Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09398)
*Tim C. Rese,Nils Japke,Diana Baumann,Natalie Carl,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoBenchr，一个开源的、以应用为中心的时空数据库基准测试套件，用于评估PostGIS、SpaceTime、MobilityDB等时空数据库系统的性能、可扩展性和配置影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空数据库系统（如PostGIS、SpaceTime、MobilityDB）在功能和性能上差异显著，且现有的时空基准测试范围有限，缺乏以实际应用为中心的基准测试套件。

Method: 开发了GeoBenchr原型，支持多种数据集、查询类型和工作负载模式，涵盖骑行、航空和船舶跟踪等真实应用场景，并用于评估各系统的可扩展性、配置影响和跨平台性能对比。

Result: 实验结果强调了以应用为中心的基准测试在选择适合实际应用场景的时空数据库系统中的重要性。

Conclusion: GeoBenchr能够全面评估时空数据库平台，为实际应用中的系统选择提供可靠依据。

Abstract: The rapid growth of spatiotemporal data volumes needs to be handled by database systems capable of efficiently managing and querying such data. Existing systems such as PostGIS, SpaceTime, and MobilityDB offer partial solutions but differ widely in scope and performance. Also, first spatiotemporal benchmarks provide valuable insights but are limited in scope and, to our knowledge, no application-centric benchmarking suite exists. In this paper, we propose GeoBenchr, an open-source, application-centric benchmarking suite for spatiotemporal platforms. GeoBenchr enables comprehensive evaluation across diverse datasets, query types, and workload patterns, reflecting realistic use cases from domains such as cycling, aviation, and maritime tracking. We use our GeoBenchr prototype to evaluate several system aspects including scalability, configuration impact, and cross-platform performance comparison. Our results highlight the importance of application-centric benchmarking in selecting suitable spatiotemporal database systems for real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [30] [No Cliques Allowed: The Next Step Towards BDD/FC Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09558)
*Lucas Larroque,Piotr Ostropolski-Nalewaja,Michaël Thomazo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文证明了有界推导深度(bdd)规则集生成的通用模型不能包含任意大的竞赛图(完全有向图)而不产生循环查询，从而向解决bdd⇒fc猜想迈出了一步，缩小了潜在反例的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决存在规则领域中关于有界推导深度规则集有限可控性(bdd⇒fc)的猜想，这是一个基本的开放问题。

Method: 证明通用模型不能包含任意大的竞赛图而不蕴含循环查询∃x E(x,x)。

Result: 表明bdd规则集生成的通用模型在包含任意大竞赛图时必然导致循环查询，从而排除了这类结构作为反例的可能性。

Conclusion: 这一简洁而优雅的结果缩小了bdd⇒fc猜想的潜在反例空间，为该猜想的肯定解决提供了进展。

Abstract: This paper addresses one of the fundamental open questions in the realm of existential rules: the conjecture on the finite controllability of bounded derivation depth rule sets (bdd $\Rightarrow$ fc). We take a step toward a positive resolution of this conjecture by demonstrating that universal models generated by bdd rule sets cannot contain arbitrarily large tournaments (arbitrarily directed cliques) without entailing a loop query, $\exists{x} E(x, x)$. This simple yet elegant result narrows the space of potential counterexamples to the (bdd $\Rightarrow$ fc) conjecture.

</details>


### [31] [Local Stability of Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09724)
*Felix S. Campbell,Yuval Moskovitch*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的排名稳定性度量方法——局部稳定性(local stability)，用于衡量对排名项目中值的微小修改对其排名的影响。该方法还考虑了排名中具有相似质量的项目集合（密集区域）的存在。论文证明该问题的计算一般是困难的，并提出了基于采样的近似算法LStability和密集区域检测算法Detect-Dense-Region。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注算法变化对排名稳定性的影响，但缺乏衡量数据本身微小变化对排名影响的度量方法。当项目的微小值变化导致排名显著改变时，会影响决策质量。因此需要一种能够捕捉这种敏感性的度量。

Method: 1. 提出局部稳定性定义，考虑值变化对排名的影响，同时允许密集区域内的项目互换位置；2. 证明该问题的计算困难性；3. 提出LStability算法，基于采样和浓度不等式提供PAC型近似保证；4. 提出Detect-Dense-Region算法检测项目所在密集区域；5. 引入多种优化提高算法可扩展性和效率。

Result: 理论证明计算局部稳定性一般困难；提出近似算法并具有PAC保证；通过广泛实验验证框架有效性，包括展示定义实用性的案例研究。

Conclusion: 论文提供了评估排名对数据变化敏感性的新框架，局部稳定性可帮助识别哪些项目的排名是稳健的，哪些对微小变化敏感，为决策提供重要参考。

Abstract: Rankings play a crucial role in decision-making. However, if minor changes to items significantly alter their rankings, the quality of the decisions being made can be compromised. The stability of ranking is a measure used to assess how modifications to the ranking algorithm or data affect results. While previous work has focused on stability of the ranking under changes to the algorithm, we introduce a novel measure we refer to as local stability. Local stability indicates the effect of minor changes to the values of an item in the ranking on its rank. Our proposed definition furthermore takes into account the presence of multiple items with similar qualities in the ranking, called dense regions, permitting minor modifications to swap the positions of items within the region. We show that computing this measure in general is hard, and in turn propose a relaxation of the definition to admit approximation.
  We present (i) LStability, a sampling-based algorithm for approximating local stability, on which we make probably-approximately-correct-type guarantees through the use of concentration inequalities, and (ii) Detect-Dense-Region, an algorithm based on this approach to detect the dense region an item lies in, if it exists. We introduce a number of optimizations to our algorithms to improve their scalability and efficiency. We validate our proposed framework through an extensive suite of experiments, including case studies highlighting the utility of our definitions.

</details>


### [32] [Epistemic Closure: Autonomous Mechanism Completion for Physically Consistent Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09756)
*Yue Wua,Tianhao Su,Rui Hu,Mingchuan Zhao,Shunbo Hu,Deng Pan,Jizhong Huang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号生成智能体，作为传统数值引擎的认知监督器，通过将物理定律封装为模块化本构技能，利用潜在内在先验和思维链推理，自主验证、修剪和完善物理机制，解决大语言模型在科学发现中的隐式上下文问题和物理幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型(LLM)集成到科学发现中受到隐式上下文问题的阻碍：从文献中提取的控制方程包含不可见的热力学假设（如排水条件），标准生成模型无法识别，导致物理幻觉——生成语法正确但物理无效的求解器。

Method: 提出神经符号生成智能体(Neuro-Symbolic Generative Agent)：(1)将物理定律封装为模块化本构技能(Constitutive Skills)；(2)利用潜在内在先验(latent intrinsic priors)；(3)采用思维链(Chain-of-Thought)推理工作流；(4)自主验证、修剪和完善物理机制。

Result: 在低渗透率砂岩热增压问题的演示中：标准文献检索基线错误预测灾难性材料失效（盲目采用刚性"不排水"简化）；智能体通过无量纲尺度分析自主识别系统处于排水条件(Deborah数De<<1)；归纳补全缺失的耗散机制(Darcy流)以满足边界约束；预测与实验一致的稳定应力路径。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了AI智能体超越编码助手角色作为认知伙伴的范式，能够推理和纠正科学数据中嵌入的理论假设，为AI辅助科学发现开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into scientific discovery is currently hindered by the Implicit Context problem, where governing equations extracted from literature contain invisible thermodynamic assumptions (e.g., undrained conditions) that standard generative models fail to recognize. This leads to Physical Hallucination: the generation of syntactically correct solvers that faithfully execute physically invalid laws. Here, we introduce a Neuro-Symbolic Generative Agent that functions as a cognitive supervisor atop traditional numerical engines. By encapsulating physical laws into modular Constitutive Skills and leveraging latent intrinsic priors, the Agent employs a Chain-of-Thought reasoning workflow to autonomously validate, prune, and complete physical mechanisms. We demonstrate this capability on the challenge of thermal pressurization in low-permeability sandstone. While a standard literature-retrieval baseline erroneously predicts catastrophic material failure by blindly adopting a rigid "undrained" simplification, our Agent autonomously identifies the system as operating in a drained regime (Deborah number De << 1) via dimensionless scaling analysis. Consequently, it inductively completes the missing dissipation mechanism (Darcy flow) required to satisfy boundary constraints, predicting a stable stress path consistent with experimental reality. This work establishes a paradigm where AI agents transcend the role of coding assistants to act as epistemic partners, capable of reasoning about and correcting the theoretical assumptions embedded in scientific data.

</details>


### [33] [Expressive Power of Property Graph Constraint Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09806)
*Stefania Dumbrava,Nadime Francis,Victor Marsault,Steven Sailly*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文首次对属性图约束语言的表现力进行了系统研究，重点关注PG-Keys语言，并将其与图函数依赖(GFD)和图生成依赖(GGG)进行比较，建立了完整的表现力层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动GQL标准修订进程，需明确PG-Keys与现有属性图约束形式化方法的相对位置和表现力差异。不同形式化方法允许不同类型的图模式语言和数据谓词，需要统一框架进行比较。

Method: 基于结构差异建立统一框架，使用带等式和不等式谓词的结合正则路径查询(CRPQ)作为图模式；识别良好行为片段，建立表现力包含关系，证明分离结果。

Result: 建立了完整且严格的表现力层次结构，精确确定了PG-Keys何时提供严格更大的表现力，阐明了PG-Keys在现有属性图约束形式化方法中的地位。

Conclusion: 首次对属性图约束语言的表现力进行了原则性和系统性研究，为GQL标准的修订提供了理论依据，明确了PG-Keys与GFD、GGD之间的表现力关系。

Abstract: We present the first principled and systematic study of the expressive power of property graph constraint languages, focused on the recent PG-Keys language, set to inform the upcoming revision of the GQL standard. To this end, we position PG-Keys within the broader landscape of existing formalisms. In particular, we compare PG-Keys with two core property graph constraint languages: Graph Functional Dependencies (GFD) and Graph Generating Dependencies (GGD). One hurdle is that these formalisms allow different kinds of graph pattern languages and data predicates. To make a fair comparison, based on their structural differences only, we first present a unifying framework. Within this framework, we consider conjunctive regular path queries (CRPQ) as graph patterns with equality and inequality predicates. We then identify well-behaved fragments, establish expressiveness inclusion, and prove separation results, yielding a complete and strict hierarchy of expressive power. The results identify precisely when PG-Keys provide strictly greater expressive power, clarifying their place among state-of-the-art property graph constraint formalisms.

</details>


### [34] [How to Write to SSDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09927)
*Bohyun Lee,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了SSD数据库的异地写优化，通过重新设计LeanStore B树结构，显著提升了OLTP性能并大幅减少了flash写入，为支持新型SSD接口（ZNS/FDP）提供了架构基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采用异地写对于数据库系统充分发挥SSD性能并延长SSD寿命至关重要。

Method: 提出一组跨DBMS和SSD层的异地写优化以减少写放大；将原地更新的B树结构LeanStore重新设计为支持异地写入，并支持这些优化；在多种OLTP基准测试、数据集大小和SSD上进行评估。

Result: 在YCSB-A上，吞吐量提升1.65-2.24倍，每事务flash写入减少6.2-9.8倍；在TPC-C 15000仓库配置下，吞吐量提升2.45倍，flash写入减少7.2倍。

Conclusion: 该架构可无缝支持ZNS和FDP等新型SSD接口。通过采用异地写优化，数据库系统能够充分发挥SSD性能并延长SSD寿命。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates that adopting out-of-place writes is essential for database systems to fully leverage SSD performance and extend SSD lifespan. We propose a set of out-of-place optimizations that collectively reduce write amplification across both the DBMS and SSD layers. We redesign the in-place, B-tree-based LeanStore to write out-of-place and support these optimizations, and evaluate it on diverse OLTP benchmarks, dataset sizes, and SSDs. The final design improves throughput by 1.65-2.24x and reduces flash writes per transaction by 6.2-9.8x on YCSB-A. On TPC-C with 15,000 warehouses, throughput improves by 2.45x while flash writes decrease by 7.2x. Finally, we show that the architecture can seamlessly support novel SSD interfaces such as ZNS and FDP.

</details>

### [35] [Hindsight Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08754)
*Hui-Ze Tan,Xiao-Wen Yang,Hao Chen,Jie-Jing Shao,Yi Wen,Yuteng Shen,Weihong Luo,Xiku Du,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HCAPO通过集成后见信用分配机制解决LLM智能体在长程任务中的信用分配问题，利用LLM作为事后批评者细化Q值，并采用多尺度优势机制补充不准确的价值基线，在WebShop和ALFWorld上分别实现7.7%和13.8%的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长程多步任务中面临显著的信用分配挑战，原因是奖励稀疏。现有无价值方法（如GRPO）存在两个根本性瓶颈：(1) 不准确的步级Q值估计；(2) 中间状态的价值基线不对齐。

Method: HCAPO框架包含两个核心创新：(1) 利用LLM本身作为事后批评者，通过后见推理来细化步级Q值；(2) 多尺度优势机制，在关键决策状态下有效补充不准确的价值基线。

Result: HCAPO在三个具有挑战性的基准测试（包括WebShop和ALFWorld）上持续优于最先进的RL方法。使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型，HCAPO在WebShop上实现了7.7%的成功率提升，在ALFWorld上实现了13.8%的提升。

Conclusion: HCAPO是首个将后见信用分配集成到LLM智能体中的框架，通过利用LLM本身作为事后批评者来细化步级Q值，并通过多尺度优势机制补充不准确的价值基线。实验表明，HCAPO在WebShop上实现7.7%的成功率提升，在ALFWorld上实现13.8%的提升，证明了其在复杂长程任务中显著增强探索效率、促进简洁决策并确保可扩展性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents often face significant credit assignment challenges in long-horizon, multi-step tasks due to sparse rewards. Existing value-free methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), encounter two fundamental bottlenecks: inaccurate step-level Q-value estimation and misaligned value baselines for intermediate states. To address these limitations, we introduce HCAPO, the first framework to integrate hindsight credit assignment into LLM agents. HCAPO leverages the LLM itself as a post-hoc critic to refine step-level Q-values through hindsight reasoning. Furthermore, HCAPO's multi-scale advantage mechanism effectively supplements the inaccurate value baselines at critical decision states. Evaluations across three challenging benchmarks, including WebShop and ALFWorld, demonstrate that HCAPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art RL methods. Notably, HCAPO achieves a 7.7% improvement in success rate on WebShop and a 13.8% on ALFWorld over GRPO using the Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model. These results indicate that HCAPO significantly enhances exploration efficiency, promotes concise decision-making, and ensures scalability in complex, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [36] [Generalized Reduction to the Isotropy for Flexible Equivariant Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08758)
*Alejandro García-Castellanos,Gijs Bellaard,Remco Duits,Daniel Pelt,Erik J Bekkers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当群G在空间M上传递作用时，乘积空间X×M上的G不变函数可以约化为等距子群H在X上的不变量，建立了轨道等价(X×M)/G≅X/H，并将其应用于等变神经场以扩展到任意群作用和齐次条件空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何学习问题常需处理异构乘积空间（携带不同群作用的不同空间的乘积），现有标准技术无法直接应用；等变神经场方法存在结构性限制。

Method: 利用群作用的传递性和等距子群建立显式轨道等价(X×M)/G≅X/H，实现保持表达能力的原则性约化。

Result: 将等变神经场扩展到任意群作用和齐次条件空间，移除了现有方法的主要结构约束。

Conclusion: 该工作为异构乘积空间上的不变函数提供了统一的理论基础，并拓宽了等变神经场的应用范围。

Abstract: Many geometric learning problems require invariants on heterogeneous product spaces, i.e., products of distinct spaces carrying different group actions, where standard techniques do not directly apply. We show that, when a group $G$ acts transitively on a space $M$, any $G$-invariant function on a product space $X \times M$ can be reduced to an invariant of the isotropy subgroup $H$ of $M$ acting on $X$ alone. Our approach establishes an explicit orbit equivalence $(X \times M)/G \cong X/H$, yielding a principled reduction that preserves expressivity. We apply this characterization to Equivariant Neural Fields, extending them to arbitrary group actions and homogeneous conditioning spaces, and thereby removing the major structural constraints imposed by existing methods.

5. 经验表明RQRE-OVI在自博弈中具有竞争力，在交叉博弈中产生更鲁棒的行为。

Abstract: Provably efficient and robust equilibrium computation in general-sum Markov games remains a core challenge in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Nash equilibrium is computationally intractable in general and brittle due to equilibrium multiplicity and sensitivity to approximation error. We study Risk-Sensitive Quantal Response Equilibrium (RQRE), which yields a unique, smooth solution under bounded rationality and risk sensitivity. We propose \texttt{RQRE-OVI}, an optimistic value iteration algorithm for computing RQRE with linear function approximation in large or continuous state spaces. Through finite-sample regret analysis, we establish convergence and explicitly characterize how sample complexity scales with rationality and risk-sensitivity parameters. The regret bounds reveal a quantitative tradeoff: increasing rationality tightens regret, while risk sensitivity induces regularization that enhances stability and robustness. This exposes a Pareto frontier between expected performance and robustness, with Nash recovered in the limit of perfect rationality and risk neutrality. We further show that the RQRE policy map is Lipschitz continuous in estimated payoffs, unlike Nash, and RQRE admits a distributionally robust optimization interpretation. Empirically, we demonstrate that \texttt{RQRE-OVI} achieves competitive performance under self-play while producing substantially more robust behavior under cross-play compared to Nash-based approaches. These results suggest \texttt{RQRE-OVI} offers a principled, scalable, and tunable path for equilibrium learning with improved robustness and generalization.

</details>
